ReLAT operationalizes this principle by constructing a differentiable Question -> Latent Thought -> Question cycle and optimizing query reconstruction loss through the latent thought before answer generation. This anchors opaque latent computation to the problem specification it is supposed to represent. Across mathematical reasoning, knowledge QA, and code generation benchmarks on the Qwen family, ReLAT consistently improves over single-model inference, text-based collaboration, open-loop latent collaboration, and alternative test-time training objectives. On Qwen3-8B, ReLAT raises AIME 2024 accuracy from 56.7% to 73.3%, a 16.6-point gain over the strongest open-loop latent baseline.

</details>


### [292] [RedKnot: Efficient Long-Context LLM Serving with Head-Aware KV Reuse and SegPagedAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06256)
*Yang Liu,ZhaoKai Luo,HuaYi Jin,ZhiYong Wang,RuoZhou He,BoYu Wang,Guanjie Chen,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RedKnot 提出一种面向注意力头的 KV cache 管理系统，将传统单体 KV cache 拆解为按 KV head 组织的结构化内存对象，以提升长上下文 LLM 推理服务中的内存效率、复用能力和分布式扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 服务输入长度增长，KV cache 成为 GPU 显存容量、并发能力、缓存复用和分布式扩展的主要瓶颈。现有系统通常把 KV cache 视为同质的 token 级内存块序列，忽略不同 KV heads 在功能角色、注意力距离和运行时重要性上的结构化差异，导致资源利用不充分。

Method: 论文提出 RedKnot，通过按 KV heads 分解 KV cache，根据不同 head 的重要性和有效注意力范围进行差异化管理。该表示将 KV cache 从单体张量抽象转化为结构化内存对象，并统一支持位置无关 KV 复用、前缀 KV 压缩、冷热 KV 分离和分布式 KV 放置，无需模型重训练或微调。

Result: 摘要宣称 RedKnot 在保持输出保真度的同时提升资源效率，并改善长上下文 LLM serving 中的缓存复用、压缩、冷热分层和分布式管理能力。但摘要未给出具体实验指标、数据集、模型规模或性能提升数值。

Conclusion: RedKnot 的核心贡献是把 KV cache 从被动、单体的运行时产物转变为动态、模型感知的运行时基础设施，为可扩展 LLM 服务提供新的 KV cache 管理范式。

Abstract: As the input length of large language model (LLM) serving continues to grow, the KV cache has become a dominant bottleneck in AI infrastructure. It limits GPU memory capacity, serving concurrency, cache reuse, and distributed scalability. Several important problems, including position-independent KV cache, prefix KV cache compression, hot/cold KV cache separation, and distributed KV cache management, all depend on how the KV cache is represented and managed. However, existing serving systems largely rely on a monolithic KV cache abstraction, where the KV cache is treated as a homogeneous sequence of token-level memory blocks and managed with similar policies across attention heads and serving scenarios. We observe that KV cache utility is highly structured across KV heads: different heads exhibit different functional roles, attention distances, and runtime importance. Therefore, a full KV cache is not always necessary for every head, token range, or serving scenario.
  We present RedKnot, a head-aware KV cache management system for LLM serving. RedKnot breaks the conventional monolithic KV cache abstraction by decomposing the KV cache along KV heads, whose importance and effective attention ranges vary significantly across serving scenarios. This head-level decomposition turns the KV cache from a monolithic tensor abstraction into a structured memory object, enabling RedKnot to uniformly support position-independent KV reuse, prefix KV compression, hot/cold KV separation, and distributed KV placement while preserving output fidelity and improving resource efficiency, without requiring model retraining or fine-tuning. RedKnot establishes a new foundation for AI infrastructure by transforming the KV cache from a monolithic, passive runtime artifact into a dynamic, model-aware runtime substrate for scalable LLM serving.

</details>


### [293] [ToolChoiceConfusion: Causal Minimal Tool Filtering for Reliable LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06284)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Causal Minimal Tool Filtering（CMTF），一种无需训练的工具筛选方法，通过因果充分性而非单纯语义相关性，仅暴露当前步骤必要的最小工具集合，在保持任务成功率的同时显著降低工具数量与 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体依赖外部工具，但工具菜单过大会导致误调用、过早行动、可靠性下降以及 token 成本增加。现有方法多基于语义相关性选择工具，但相关工具未必是当前步骤必要或时机合适的工具，因此需要一种能判断“当前是否因果必要”的筛选机制。

Method: 作者提出 CMTF：使用轻量级的前置条件-效果契约描述工具作用，根据当前状态与用户目标推断推进任务所需的最小下一步工具前沿，只向模型暴露这些工具。实验将 CMTF 与全工具暴露、关键词检索、状态感知过滤和因果路径消融等方法比较。

Result: 在包含 102 个任务、100 个工具、4 个 LLM 后端、2448 次任务-方法-模型运行的基准上，CMTF 在总体成功率上与最强因果基线相当，同时将每步可见工具从 100 个降至 1 个，并相对全工具暴露减少约 90% 的 token 使用。

Conclusion: 仅依赖语义相关性不足以实现高效可靠的工具使用；基于因果充分性的最小工具筛选能在不训练模型的情况下显著降低工具暴露和成本，同时维持较高任务成功率。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on external tools, but larger tool menus can reduce reliability and efficiency by increasing wrong-tool calls, premature actions, and token cost. Existing tool-selection methods often optimize semantic relevance, exposing tools whose names or descriptions match the user request. We argue that relevance is insufficient: a tool may be related to the task while still being unnecessary or premature at the current step.
  We propose Causal Minimal Tool Filtering (CMTF), a training-free method that selects tools by causal sufficiency. CMTF uses lightweight precondition-effect contracts to expose only the minimal next-step tool frontier needed to advance from the current state toward the user goal. Across multi-step tool-use tasks, we compare CMTF with all-tools exposure, keyword retrieval, state-aware filtering, and causal-path ablations, measuring task success, wrong-tool calls, premature actions, tool exposure, and token cost. In the main benchmark with 102 tasks, 100 tools, four LLM backends, and 2448 task-method-model runs, CMTF matches the strongest causal baseline in aggregate success while reducing visible tools from 100 to one per step and reducing token usage by about 90% relative to all-tools exposure.

</details>


### [294] [TRACE: A Temporal Conditional Estimation for Multimodal Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06285)
*Ziwen Kan,Yishuo Chen,Kecheng Li,Andrew Wen,Xiaomeng Wang,Liwei Wang,Jihao Duan,Song Wang,Hongfang Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRACE，用于在多模态时间序列基础模型中处理时间不对齐、 irregular sampling 与模态缺失问题，通过利用辅助模态条件估计缺失目标模态，提升下游任务鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多模态时间序列常存在不同模态采样频率不一致、时间错位以及部分模态缺失；现有简单插补或掩码方法难以建模跨模态依赖，导致表征退化。

Method: 提出 TRACE 条件估计范式，在缺失与不规则采样场景下，从可用辅助模态系统性推断不完整目标模态，并将其用于多模态时间序列基础模型管线中的表征学习与下游预测。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV、CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI 等多模态基准及多种缺失模态设置下，TRACE 相比既有多模态融合方法表现更优，尤其在严重模态缺失时更稳健。

Conclusion: TRACE 能有效缓解多模态时间序列中的缺失与时间不对齐问题，学习更可靠的跨模态表征，并提升多类下游任务性能。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TS-FMs) aim to learn generalizable temporal representations that can be adapted to a wide range of downstream tasks. In real-world multimodal settings, time series are frequently affected by temporal misalignment and partial modality missingness, where different modalities are observed at heterogeneous time scales or are partially absent. Existing approaches typically rely on naive imputation or masking strategies, which fail to account for cross-modal dependencies and often lead to misaligned or degraded representations. We propose TRACE, a conditional estimation paradigm for multimodal time series foundation model pipelines under missingness and irregular sampling, allowing incomplete target modalities to be systematically inferred from available auxiliary modalities. We evaluate TRACE on diverse multimodal benchmarks spanning healthcare and affective computing, including the MIMIC-IV clinical dataset and the CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI benchmarks for multimodal sentiment analysis. Across a range of downstream prediction tasks and missing-modality settings, TRACE consistently outperforms prior multimodal fusion approaches, demonstrating improved robustness to severe modality missingness and more reliable cross-modal representations.

</details>


### [295] [Multi-ResNets for Subspace Preconditioning in Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06300)
*Merve Karakas,Christopher J. Williams,Emmanuel O. Balogun,Sadegh Sadeghi Tabas,Christian Brown,Nikhil Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MResOpt：一种用于约束优化的分阶段残差神经网络，在 predict-complete-correct 流水线中按优先级逐步满足约束，在多类优化基准与 AC 最优潮流任务上降低高优先级约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型约束优化方法往往难以同时高效满足多类约束，尤其当约束具有天然优先级或物理结构时，普通投影/修正策略未充分利用这种有序结构，导致关键约束违反较大。

Method: 设计分阶段残差网络 MResOpt，通过中间 re-completion、stage-aware losses 和按优先级分解的约束修正机制，在 predict-complete-correct 框架内实现有序约束满足；理论上在无限宽理想化条件下分析其等价于顺序高斯过程回归。

Result: 在合成 QP、QCQP、SOCP 基准中，该分阶段架构在凸与非凸场景均提升高优先级约束满足；在带线路潮流约束的 AC 最优潮流中，结合物理启发的约束排序，相比重新投影基线显著降低高优先级违反且保持计算效率。

Conclusion: MResOpt 表明，将约束优先级和领域结构显式融入神经优化架构，可以改善关键约束可行性，并支持模块化的“分工式”学习修正过程，适合结构化约束优化问题。

Abstract: We propose MResOpt, a staged residual neural network architecture for constrained optimization problems. Our architecture fits within predict-complete-correct pipelines and decomposes constraint satisfaction by priority through intermediate re-completion and stage-aware losses. The framework enables domain-informed ordered constraint satisfaction which allows the network to utilize ordinal structure when present. Under an idealized infinite-width regime, we show that our design behaves as sequential Gaussian Process regression. On synthetic QP, QCQP, and SOCP benchmarks, the staged architecture improves high-priority constraint satisfaction across convex and non-convex settings. On line-flow-constrained AC optimal power flow, we introduce a physics-motivated constraint ordering and show that MResOpt supports a learned division of labor that keeps iterates on the equality manifold, achieving substantially lower high-priority violation than reprojected baselines while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [296] [AIS-Based Vessel Trajectory Prediction Using Memory-Augmented Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06311)
*Wonmo Koo,Sanha Chang,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究基于外部记忆的船舶轨迹预测方法，并在AIS数据上验证其相较无外部记忆深度学习基线具有稳定且显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测船舶轨迹对海事安全、避碰和航线优化至关重要；尽管记忆增强神经网络已在行人和道路车辆轨迹预测中表现良好，但其在船舶轨迹预测中的应用仍缺乏充分探索。

Method: 使用自动识别系统（AIS）数据，对记忆型轨迹预测模型进行实证研究；该类模型通过外部记忆选择性检索相关历史信息，并与不使用外部记忆的多种深度学习基线进行比较。

Result: 在Gulf of Mexico和New York Bight数据集上的实验显示，记忆增强方法在多个设置下均持续、显著优于不包含外部记忆的深度学习基线。

Conclusion: 外部记忆机制能够有效提升船舶轨迹预测性能，说明记忆增强神经网络在海事轨迹建模中具有较大应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate vessel trajectory prediction is essential for safe and efficient maritime operations, enabling collision avoidance and supporting route optimization. Although memory-augmented neural networks have recently shown strong performance in pedestrian and road-vehicle trajectory prediction by selectively retrieving relevant information from an external memory, their potential for vessel trajectory prediction remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical investigation of memory-based trajectory prediction using Automatic Identification System (AIS) data. Experiments on data from the Gulf of Mexico and the New York Bight demonstrate consistent and substantial performance gains over a range of deep learning baselines that do not incorporate an external memory.

</details>


### [297] [LLM Self-Recognition: Steering and Retrieving Activation Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06315)
*Thibaud Ardoin,Jonas Schäfer,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种利用大语言模型内部表示进行文本归因的方法：在生成阶段通过随机稀疏向量干预残差流，形成可检测“指纹”，检测准确率超过98%，且不明显损害文本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容快速增加，传统外部水印或检测器可能存在鲁棒性、泛化性和质量影响问题。作者希望利用LLM自身表示空间中已有的可识别结构，实现更可靠、低干扰的模型输出归因。

Method: 首先验证LLM对自身生成文本具有稳定的自识别能力；随后在生成过程中对模型内部残差流施加随机稀疏向量 steering，诱导生成文本携带特定模型可识别的隐式信号；最后使用另一个LLM作为检测器，从其激活表示中恢复该信号，并进行多模型归因评估。

Result: 该方法在多种检测设置下达到超过98%的归因准确率，同时保持生成文本质量，尤其表明即使在低熵生成场景中，自识别与指纹恢复仍然可靠。

Conclusion: 论文认为LLM激活空间包含可利用的结构，可在不显著干扰语义和文本质量的情况下编码归因信号。这为AI文本溯源提供了不同于传统外部水印的实用路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in interpretability suggest that large language models (LLMs) implicitly encode signals in their generated text that enable self-recognition of their outputs. We demonstrate that this capability is reliable, even in low-entropy scenarios, and that it can be amplified through targeted intervention. By steering the internal residual stream during generation with a random sparse vector, we create a detectable fingerprint that enables attribution of a given text to a specific LLM. This signal is recoverable from the activations of an LLM used as a detector, achieving over 98% accuracy across multiple detection settings while preserving the quality of generated text. As AI-generated content proliferates, this approach offers a practical alternative to traditional detectors by leveraging the model's natural representation structure for attribution rather than embedding a signal externally. Our contributions include: (i) establishing reliable self-recognition capabilities in LLMs, (ii) a simple steering mechanism enabling multi-LLM identification with no quality degradation, (iii) demonstrating that activation spaces contain exploitable structure for encoding signals without semantic interference.

</details>


### [298] [DragOn: A Benchmark and Dataset for Drag-Based GUI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06322)
*Nathan Bout,Maxime Langevin,Ronan Riochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DragOn 是一个面向 GUI 智能体拖拽操作的新基准与训练数据集，显著扩充了拖拽 grounding 数据规模，并显示可提升模型在下游计算机使用任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 智能体在点击定位方面受益于百万级数据集而进展明显，但拖拽类交互（如拖放、滑动、文本高亮、区域选择等）数据规模不足，导致模型在复杂拖拽操作上的能力仍然有限。

Method: 作者构建 DragOn 数据集与评测基准，覆盖文本高亮、单元格选择、元素缩放和滑块操控四类任务；数据包含 28.6 万张训练截图、350 万个训练任务，以及 2000 个保留评测样本。论文评估了 GPT、Claude 等闭源模型，Qwen、Kimi、Holo 等开源权重模型，并进一步对 Qwen 视觉语言模型进行数据微调。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有模型在复杂拖拽 grounding 上仍有不足；使用 DragOn 训练数据微调后的模型表现有所提升，并且该数据集可能增强当前先进模型在下游计算机使用任务中的能力。

Conclusion: DragOn 弥补了 GUI 智能体拖拽操作数据稀缺的问题，为拖拽 grounding 提供了大规模训练资源和标准化评测集，有助于推动 GUI 自动化模型在更复杂交互场景中的发展。

Abstract: GUI agents - vision-based models that control desktops, web browsers, and mobile devices through graphical user interfaces - promise to automate a wide range of digital tasks. While million-scale datasets have enabled substantial progress on click-grounding, drag grounding (e.g. drag-and-drop, swipe, highlight) data remains an order of magnitude smaller and current models fall short on complex drag-based interactions. We introduce DragOn, a drag grounding benchmark and training dataset covering four domains: text highlighting, cell selection, element resizing and slider manipulation. The dataset comprises 286K training screenshots and 3.5M training tasks, plus a 2000-example held-out evaluation suite. We evaluate proprietary (GPT, Claude) and open-weight (Qwen, Kimi, Holo) models, as well as a Qwen VLM fine-tuned on our training data. Results suggest that our dataset could improve performance of state-of-the-art models on downstream computer-use tasks.

</details>


### [299] [TokenMizer: Graph-Structured Session Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06337)
*Shweta Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TokenMizer 将长会话历史建模为类型化知识图谱，并通过压缩与检查点机制生成紧凑的恢复块，在更低 token 成本下提升任务、决策与文件信息的召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期 LLM 任务会超出有效上下文窗口，导致关键结构化信息被丢弃；现有方法多将历史视为扁平文本，破坏了决策、任务迁移、文件变更等关系结构，影响会话恢复能力。

Method: 提出开源代理系统 TokenMizer：使用包含 14 类节点和 7 类边的类型化知识图谱表示会话历史；通过混合抽取管线增量构图；使用三层检查点系统序列化为紧凑恢复块；结合 8 层压缩管线降低上下文开销，并使用语义缓存降低重复查询延迟。

Result: 在覆盖 5 个领域的 21 个受控会话上，TokenMizer 的恢复块平均 78 tokens（42–124），约为基线 159–170 tokens 的一半；决策召回提升 9–17 个百分点。总体平均任务召回 51.0%、决策召回 46.6%、文件召回 58.7%。消融显示模糊标签匹配是主要增益来源，任务召回提升 33 个百分点；启发式压缩在零外部依赖下减少 47.3% token。

Conclusion: TokenMizer 相比文本保留式基线，以更低 token 成本保留更丰富的结构化会话信息，尤其能保存技术选择背后的理由，为长周期 LLM 会话恢复提供了可查询、可压缩的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) deployments for long-horizon tasks face a fundamental constraint: context windows are finite while productive work sessions are not. When history exceeds the Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW), critical structured information - architectural decisions, task transitions, file histories - is silently discarded. Existing mitigations treat history as flat text, destroying the relational structure that makes sessions resumable. We present TokenMizer, an open-source proxy system that models LLM session history as a typed knowledge graph. The schema defines 14 node types and 7 edge types. A hybrid extraction pipeline populates the graph incrementally, while a three-tier checkpoint system serializes it into compact resume blocks. An 8-layer compression pipeline reduces context overhead, and a semantic cache reduces repeated-query latency. Evaluated on a controlled benchmark of 21 sessions spanning 5 domains, TokenMizer demonstrates significant token economy. It produces resume blocks averaging 78 tokens (range: 42-124) - 2x smaller than evaluated baselines (159-170 tokens) - while achieving higher decision recall (+9-17 percentage points). Crucially, baselines only preserve that a technology was mentioned; TokenMizer preserves the rationale. Across all sessions, TokenMizer achieves mean task recall 51.0%, decision recall 46.6%, and file recall 58.7%. Variance reflects domain heterogeneity: explicit imperative phrasing (software engineering) scores higher than implicit reasoning (research). Ablation studies show fuzzy label matching is the dominant improvement factor (+33 pp task recall). The heuristic compression achieves 47.3% token reduction with zero external dependencies. TokenMizer provides a queryable alternative to text-retention baselines at half the token cost.

</details>


### [300] [Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06345)
*Yohann Benchetrit,Marlène Careil,Simon Dahan,Hubert Banville,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究用预训练 fMRI 响应生成模型产生合成神经数据，以增强小规模 fMRI 数据集中的脑解码训练；在两个数据集上，Top-10 图像检索准确率最高提升 68%，并显示一定零样本潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑解码依赖大量带标签神经数据，但 fMRI 数据采集成本高、样本有限，低数据量场景下性能受限。因此作者希望验证：预训练的大规模 fMRI 编码模型生成的合成数据，是否能提升小数据集上的图像解码效果，以及在什么条件下有效。

Method: 作者使用在超过 1000 小时视频、音频和语言刺激 fMRI 响应上预训练的 TRIBE v2 模型，为不同数据集生成合成 fMRI 数据；随后在 7T Natural Scenes Dataset 和 3T BOLD5000 上系统改变真实数据与合成数据的训练比例，评估图像解码器的 Top-10 图像检索准确率。

Result: 与仅使用真实数据训练相比，加入合成 fMRI 数据后 Top-10 图像检索准确率最高提升 68%。不同数据源达到同等解码性能所需的合成数据比例不同。部分情况下，仅用合成 fMRI 训练的图像解码器也能取得高于随机水平的表现。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练 fMRI 响应模型可作为提升图像脑解码数据效率的基础工具；合成神经数据在低数据量场景中具有显著增益，但其最佳使用比例需根据目标数据集特性调整。

Abstract: Brain decoding is limited by the availability of labeled neural data, and remains challenging in low-data regimes. To address this issue, we investigate whether and when brain decoding can be boosted by augmenting small fMRI datasets with synthetic data generated by a pretrained model of fMRI responses to stimuli. We use TRIBE v2, a large encoding model pretrained on more than 1000 hours of fMRI responses to video, audio and language. For each dataset, we evaluate systematic grids that show how the performance of image decoders varies with the amount of synthetic data used for training. Our results, based on two datasets (the 7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset and 3T fMRI BOLD5000), show up to 68% improvement in Top-10 image-retrieval accuracy compared to decoders trained only on real data. Importantly, the proportion of augmented data required to reach a given image decoding performance needs to be adjusted depending on the data source. Surprisingly, image decoders trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can perform above chance in some settings, suggesting that TRIBE v2 can support zero-shot brain-to-image decoding. Together, these results show how large-scale models of the fMRI responses to sight, sound and language may provide a foundation to improve the data efficiency for image decoding.

</details>


### [301] [Where Should Knowledge Enter? A Layered Framework for Knowledge Infusion in Multimodal Iterative Generative Mo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06356)
*Renjith Prasad,Chathurangi Shyalika,Anushka Pawar,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模态迭代生成模型中的知识注入统一理解为“干预层”问题，并在扩散模型中验证多层组合能显著减少违背知识约束的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生成模型虽能生成流畅内容，但在需要遵循结构化、领域特定或安全关键知识时仍不可靠；现有方法多按技术手段分类，缺乏从生成过程被修改组件出发的统一分析框架。

Method: 作者将迭代生成过程视为内部状态轨迹，提出四类干预层：表层、轨迹层、潜变量层和参数层；在扩散模型中映射代表性方法，并在两个扩散骨干模型上，基于多模态知识图谱进行受控对齐实验，累积实现表层输入/输出干预与生成中期的轨迹—潜变量干预。

Result: 实验显示，每增加一层干预都能处理前一层无法覆盖的失败类型；与原始生成相比，违背知识约束的输出减少了70.97%。

Conclusion: 知识注入应被视为作用于生成过程不同结构组件的分层干预问题；多层组合具有互补性，能更有效提升多模态生成模型对结构化知识约束的遵循能力。

Abstract: Multimodal generative models produce fluent outputs but remain unreliable when generation must respect structured, domain-specific, or safety-critical knowledge. Existing methods incorporate knowledge through mechanisms such as prompt augmentation, guidance, latent editing, or fine-tuning, yet they are typically categorized by technique rather than by the component of the generative process they modify. We argue that knowledge infusion in iterative generative models is fundamentally anintervention-layer problem. Since thegenerative process unfolds as a trajectory of internal states, knowledge can act on four structurally distinct components of this process: the input/output boundary, the transition function, the intermediate state, and the model parameters. This maps to four intervention layers: surface, trajectory, latent, and parametric infusion. We instantiate the framework in diffusion models, map representative methods to all four layers, and derive design principles for multi-layer composition. In a controlled safety-alignment experiment using a multimodal knowledge graph with two diffusion backbones, we implement three of the four layers cumulatively, surface (input-side and output-side) and trajectory--latent (mid-generation). We show empirically that each additional layer addresses failure classes that prior layers cannot reach, reducing knowledge-violating outputs by 70.97% compared to vanilla generation and empirically confirming the framework's complementarity prediction.

</details>


### [302] [An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06360)
*Yonchanok Khaokaew,Ruochen Kong,Andreas Zufle,Hao Xue,Taylor Anderson,Chandini Raina MacIntyre,Matthew Scotch,Flora D. Salim,David J Heslop*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个空间化的基于代理的仿真框架，将大语言模型生成的个体自我报告流感样症状决策嵌入基于人口普查的合成人群中，用于分析疾病暴发期间行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解传染病暴发期间个体决策和行为动态，对设计有效公共卫生干预至关重要。既有研究表明LLM可基于人口学提示和情境信息模拟较真实的人类行为，但对地理空间异质性与社会人口结构的整合仍不足。

Method: 构建空间化agent-based simulation框架：利用真实人口普查数据生成城市内不同空间单元的合成人群；为代理分配人口学属性和位置；通过LLM生成其关于流感样症状自我报告的决策；比较独立推理、家庭影响、信息框架三种决策场景，并在两个城市案例中模拟报告结果。

Result: 结果显示，收入和教育水平是自我报告率差异的主要驱动因素；地理位置、LLM模型选择和信息框架也产生较小但稳定的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架能够生成同时体现社会与地理异质性的合成行为数据，可支持空间流行病学建模，并有助于分析公共卫生数据中的行为偏差和报告偏差。

Abstract: Modelling individual decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks is crucial for understanding behavioural dynamics and informing effective public health interventions. Prior work has shown that large language models can simulate realistic human behaviour by generating agent decisions based on demographic prompts and situational context. We build on this foundation with a spatially grounded, agent-based simulation framework that integrates LLM-generated decisions about self-reported influenza-like illness into a census-based synthetic population of agents. Location is treated as a central feature: agents are assigned to spatial units within cities, capturing the spatial distributions of different demographic groups using real-world census data and enabling geographically diverse behavioural modelling. We implement and compare three decision scenarios, independent reasoning, household influence, and message framing, and simulate self-reporting outcomes in San Francisco and Atlanta. Results reveal that income and education are the dominant drivers of reporting rate variation, with smaller but consistent effects from geography, LLM model choice, and message framing. Our framework generates synthetic data that captures both social and geographic heterogeneity, supporting spatial epidemiological modelling and bias-aware behavioural analysis.

</details>


### [303] [Rethinking Infrastructure Inspection as Image Difference Classification: A Traffic Sign Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06375)
*Ching Yau Fergus Mok,Lavindra de Silva,Varun Kumar Reja,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将道路基础设施缺陷检测重构为图像差异分类（IDC），以在标注数据有限的情况下支持数字孪生资产状态更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生可推动道路基础设施巡检数字化，但实际应用受限于高质量标注数据不足，尤其是低资源场景下的交通标志等资产检测。

Method: 利用连续资产状态监测中的关系性信息，将传统基于单张图像的缺陷检测转化为待检图像与参考图像之间的差异分类任务；在新构建的高质量低资源交通标志巡检数据集上，比较不同IDC分类器，包括指令式分类器和编码器式分类器。

Result: 实验显示，指令式分类器优于编码器式分类器；引入参考图像进行对比能够提升分类效果。

Conclusion: IDC是一种有效的任务建模方式，可缓解基础设施巡检中的数据依赖问题，并支持数字孪生中的资产状态更新。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) allow the digitalization of road infrastructure inspection, though this is hindered by limited annotated data. This work exploits the relational nature of continuous asset condition monitoring to reformulate image-based defect detection as image difference classification (IDC) to reduce data reliance. This was evaluated in a case study on low-resource traffic sign inspection with different IDC classifiers using a newly-curated, high quality dataset. Results indicate that the instruction-based classifier outperforms encoder-based ones and gains from comparison with reference images. This shows that IDC can be an effective task modeling for tackling data constraints in infrastructure inspection and DT asset condition updating.

</details>


### [304] [Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
*Jiaju Chen,Yuxuan Lu,Jiayi Su,Chaoran Chen,Songlin Xiao,Zheng Zhang,Yun Wang,Yunyao Li,Jian Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ALMANAC 数据集，用于研究和评估 LLM 智能体在协作过程中预测人类行为与推断行动级心理模型的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体虽具备推理、规划和工具使用等能力，但多以任务完成为优化目标，缺乏对协作过程中自我推理、伙伴意图和共同目标等心理模型的持续维护与对齐能力；同时社区缺少带有行动级心理模型标注的真实人类协作数据。

Method: 作者基于社会科学中的 Map Task 双人路径规划任务构建 ALMANAC 数据集，收集 2,987 个协作行动，并为每个行动配套理论驱动的心理模型标注，包括参与者的自我推理、对伙伴意图的感知以及对团队目标的感知；同时用六个 LLM 进行基准测试，评估其预测人类下一轮行为和心理模型的能力。

Result: 实验表明，ALMANAC 可用于评估模型模拟人类协作行为以及推断底层心理模型的能力，并揭示当前模型在过程级协作理解方面仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: ALMANAC 为构建和评估具备过程级协作能力的 LLM 智能体提供了数据基础，有助于推动智能体从单纯任务完成转向更接近人类协作者的心理模型对齐与协作推理。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

</details>


### [305] [Risk Assessment of Autonomous Driving: Integrating Technical Failures, Ethical Dilemmas, and Policy Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06396)
*Boyi Chen,Shengqin Chu,Zicheng Wang,Brian Baetz,Zhen Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于事故、接管、伦理决策与法规比较数据，指出自动驾驶风险主要集中在感知/分类技术失效、伦理框架分歧与监管不一致，并主张采用工程标准、伦理讨论和制度监管相结合的协同治理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶有望减少由人为错误导致的道路事故，但同时引入新的技术、伦理与法规风险。论文旨在系统评估这些风险及其相互关联，为大规模部署提供治理建议。

Method: 研究整合并分析多类资料来源：NHTSA公开碰撞数据、加州DMV自动驾驶接管报告、MIT Moral Machines伦理决策数据集，以及五个司法辖区的法规比较分析。

Result: 研究发现，感知与分类错误是主要技术失效模式，并在已报告事故中占比较高；自动驾驶决策存在不同伦理框架；各地区监管规则不一致增加了技术推广的不确定性。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶的技术、伦理与监管问题高度耦合，不能孤立解决。论文建议建立更具适应性和协作性的治理模式，将工程标准、伦理审议与制度监督结合起来。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technology has the potential to reduce the large number of road traffic accidents caused by human error each year, but it also brings new types of risks that need to be evaluated from the aspects of technology, ethics and regulations. Based on public crash data from the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA), disengagement reports from the California Department of Motor Vehicles (DMV), the MIT Moral Machines dataset, and a comparative regulatory analysis of five jurisdictions, we have found that the main types of technical failure modes are perception and classification errors. These account for a relatively large proportion of the reported accidents, and it can be concluded that there are different ethical frameworks for autonomous vehicle decision-making, and inconsistent regulations in different areas increase the uncertainty of widespread application. Generally speaking, the problems of technology, ethics and regulation are closely related and need to be solved together. Therefore, this paper recommends a more adaptive and cooperative governance approach that combines engineering standards, ethical discussion, and institutional supervision.

</details>


### [306] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataCOPE 是一种无监督、由验证器引导的技能发现框架，用于在推理阶段为数据分析智能体注入可复用技能，并在报告式与推理式数据分析任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时技能增强能在不更新模型参数的情况下提升数据分析智能体，但有效数据分析技能难以发现：可靠监督成本高，不同分析形式的成功标准也不一致。因此需要从无标签探索轨迹中自动发现可复用的数据分析技能。

Method: DataCOPE 通过探索轨迹生成验证信号，用于刻画轨迹间的相对质量或一致性，并迭代协调三个模块：数据分析智能体生成轨迹、无监督验证器抽取信号、技能管理器进行对比式技能蒸馏。针对报告式分析，采用自适应清单验证器，生成任务特定标准、按可验证覆盖度评分并迭代优化清单；针对推理式分析，采用答案一致性验证器，按答案一致性聚类轨迹，并将自一致性作为辅助信号。

Result: 在 Deep Data Research 的报告式分析任务和 DABStep 的推理式分析任务上，DataCOPE 均优于基线方法。四种模型设置平均来看，报告式任务平均分提升 9.71%，推理式任务平均分提升 32.30%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，即使没有人工标注监督，也可以利用探索轨迹中的验证信号发现并蒸馏可复用数据分析技能，从而有效增强数据分析智能体的泛化表现。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


### [307] [Agent Memory: Characterization and System Implications of Stateful Long-Horizon Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06448)
*Yasmine Omri,Ziyu Gan,Zachary Broveak,Robin Geens,Zexue He,Alex Pentland,Marian Verhelst,Tsachy Weissman,Thierry Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次从系统角度刻画 LLM 智能体记忆系统，提出分类法、分阶段性能分析工具，并评测十个代表系统，归纳出面向成本、延迟、更新与规模化管理的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务要求 LLM 智能体跨会话持续存储、检索和更新记忆；现有记忆系统形态多样，但其系统级行为、成本分布与设计权衡尚缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 作者提出包含四个维度的系统导向分类法；构建 phase-aware profiling harness，将成本归因到记忆构建、检索和生成阶段；在两个基准套件上评测十个代表性智能体记忆系统。

Result: 实验揭示不同设计选择会在写入路径与读取路径之间转移成本，例如构建调度、查询量摊销、记忆新鲜度与延迟之间存在显著权衡。

Conclusion: 论文总结出十条系统设计建议，覆盖构建调度、最低能力要求、按查询量摊销、 freshness-latency 权衡以及大规模智能体集群管理，为部署可扩展智能体记忆系统提供指导。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks requiring sustained reasoning over extended interaction histories. Realizing this at scale requires agents to persistently store, retrieve, and update their own memory across sessions. A rich ecosystem of agent memory systems has emerged spanning flat retrieval, LLM-mediated extraction, consolidating fact stores, and agentic control flows. Yet, their system-level behavior remains uncharacterized. We present the first systems characterization of agent memory. First, we introduce a system-oriented taxonomy classifying agent memory systems along four axes. Second, we build a phase-aware profiling harness attributing cost to construction, retrieval, and generation. Third, we characterize ten representative systems across two benchmark suites, uncovering how design choices shift cost across the write and read paths. Finally, we derive 10 system recommendations covering construction scheduling, capability floors, amortization via query volume, freshness-latency tradeoffs, and fleet-scale management.

</details>


### [308] [Vortex: Efficient and Programmable Sparse Attention Serving for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06453)
*Zhuoming Chen,Xinrui Zhong,Qilong Feng,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Yang Zhou,Michael Qizhe Shieh,Zhihao Jia,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vortex 是一个面向大语言模型服务的稀疏注意力系统，支持快速表达、部署和评估多种稀疏注意力算法，并在真实推理服务中显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 生成长度增长，稀疏注意力对降低长上下文推理成本越来越重要。但现有稀疏注意力算法从设计到大规模部署、评估需要大量工程工作，阻碍了研究者和 AI agent 对算法设计空间的快速探索。

Method: 论文提出 Vortex：前端为嵌入 Python 的领域语言，基于 page-centric tensor abstraction 表达多样稀疏注意力模式；后端与现代 LLM serving stack 深度集成，将稀疏注意力算法高效映射到实际推理系统中，支持快速原型、部署和评测。

Result: Vortex 可让 AI agents 自动生成和迭代稀疏注意力算法，最佳算法在保持精度的同时相较 full attention 获得最高 3.46 倍吞吐提升；在新兴架构和超大模型上也有效，例如 MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash 达到最高 4.7 倍吞吐提升，229B 参数 MiniMax-M2.7 在 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上达到 1.37 倍提升。

Conclusion: Vortex 将稀疏注意力的理论效率优势转化为真实服务吞吐收益，并显著降低算法探索和系统部署门槛，是推动长上下文 LLM 高效推理和自动化算法设计的重要系统工具。

Abstract: Sparse attention is becoming increasingly important for serving large language models (LLMs) as generation lengths continue to grow. However, deploying and evaluating new sparse attention algorithms at scale remains highly engineering-intensive, slowing both human researchers and AI agents in exploring the sparse attention design. To address this challenge, we present Vortex, a system that combines a Python-embedded frontend language atop a page-centric tensor abstraction for expressing a broad range of sparse attention algorithms, with an efficient backend tightly integrated into modern LLM serving stacks. Vortex enables rapid prototyping, deployment, and evaluation of sparse attention algorithms, effectively translating their theoretical efficiency gains into real-world throughput improvements. As a result, Vortex substantially accelerates the design and iteration of sparse attention algorithms. First, AI agents use Vortex to automatically generate and refine diverse algorithms, the best reaching up to $3.46\times$ higher throughput than full attention while preserving accuracy. Second, Vortex extends sparse attention to emerging architectures and very large models that are otherwise hard to experiment with, reaching up to $4.7\times$ higher throughput on the MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash and $1.37\times$ on the 229B-parameter MiniMax-M2.7 on NVIDIA B200 GPUs.

</details>


### [309] [Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06462)
*Shiyun Xiong,Dongming Wu,Peiwen Sun,Yuang Ai,Bokang Yang,Wencheng Han,Xiao-Hui Li,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Benchmark Agent，一个用于自动化构建 LLM/MLLM 评测基准的自主智能体系统，能够覆盖从需求分析到数据标注与质控的完整流程，并生成多类高质量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准构建依赖大量人工、复用困难，存在可持续性与可扩展性问题；同时许多基准发布后很快被模型刷到性能饱和，难以区分前沿模型能力差异。

Method: 作者设计了一个全自动 agentic 框架，统筹用户查询分析、子任务设计、样本生成/数据标注、质量控制等环节，并用该系统构建了15个代表性基准，覆盖文本理解、多模态理解和领域特定推理等场景。

Result: 通过人工评估、LLM-as-a-judge 评估和一致性检查，实验表明 Benchmark Agent 能以较少人工参与生成高质量评测样本；持续评测还发现当前模型在若干领域特定推理任务上仍表现不足。

Conclusion: Benchmark Agent 展示了自动化、可持续、快速迭代基准构建的可行性，有望缓解基准构建成本高与性能饱和问题，并为研究社区提供更动态、更具区分度的评测资源。

Abstract: Benchmarks are fundamental for evaluating and advancing LLMs and MLLMs by providing standardized and explicit measures of performance. However, their construction is labor-intensive and hard to reuse, raising concerns about sustainability and scalability. Moreover, existing benchmarks often quickly reach performance saturation after their release, resulting in insufficient discrimination among state-of-the-art models. To address these challenges, we introduce Benchmark Agent, a fully autonomous agentic system designed for benchmark building. Our framework orchestrates the complete benchmark construction pipeline, from user query analysis and subtask design to data annotation and quality control. To assess Benchmark Agent, we implement it to produce 15 representative benchmarks, spanning diverse evaluation scenarios, including text understanding, multimodal understanding, and domain-specific reasoning. Extensive experiments, including human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and consistency checks, demonstrate Benchmark Agent can generate high-quality benchmark samples with minimal human involvement. More importantly, through continual evaluation, we observe several insightful findings, including that current models struggle with certain domain-specific reasoning tasks. We believe that rapidly evolving benchmarks can contribute significantly to the research community. The preview and code will be publicly available at the demo page and code repository.

</details>


### [310] [Goedel-Architect: Streamlining Formal Theorem Proving with Blueprint Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06468)
*Jui-Hui Chung,Ziyang Cai,Zihao Li,Qishuo Yin,Rohit Agarwal,Simon Park,Rodrigo Porto,Narutatsu Ri,Ziran Yang,Shange Tang,Xingyu Dang,Hongzhou Lin,Mengdi Wang,Danqi Chen,Chi Jin,Liam H Fowl,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Goedel-Architect 是一个面向 Lean 4 形式化定理证明的智能体框架，通过先生成并迭代细化“蓝图”（定义与引理依赖图），再并行证明各引理，显著提升证明成功率与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流形式化证明方法多依赖递归式引理分解，容易在无效分解路径上循环或陷入死胡同，导致效率低、鲁棒性不足。论文希望通过全局依赖蓝图规划与失败驱动的蓝图修正来改善复杂定理证明。

Method: 方法分为三步：首先生成包含形式化定义、引理及依赖关系的蓝图，可选地由自然语言证明引导；其次使用带工具的 Lean prover 组件并行关闭蓝图中的开放引理节点，并利用相关依赖辅助证明；最后将失败的引理反馈给全局蓝图生成模块，驱动蓝图 refinement。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 作为骨干模型时，MiniF2F-test 达到 99.2% pass@1，PutnamBench 达到 75.6% pass@1。加入自然语言证明作为种子后，MiniF2F-test 达到 100%，PutnamBench 提升至 88.8%（597/672），并在若干高难度竞赛题集上取得较高解题率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，全局蓝图生成与失败驱动 refinement 相结合，能有效提升 Lean 4 自动形式化证明的性能与效率，并以较低成本达到开源流程中的先进水平。

Abstract: We introduce Goedel-Architect, an agentic framework for formal theorem proving in Lean 4 centered on blueprint generation and refinement. A blueprint is a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas that builds up to the main theorem. First, Goedel-Architect generates a blueprint of formally stated definitions and lemmas, along with declared dependencies. This blueprint is optionally guided by a natural language proof. Then, a tool-equipped Lean prover component closes each open lemma node in parallel using relevant dependencies. Failed lemmas in turn drive refinement of the global blueprint. This strategy contrasts with other mainstream approaches which use recursive lemma decomposition, and can inefficiently loop on dead-end strategies. Using the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) as the backbone, Goedel-Architect attains 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% pass@1 on PutnamBench. With an optional natural-language proof seeding the initial blueprint on the harder problems, we additionally close the remaining two MiniF2F-test problems (reaching 100%), lift PutnamBench to 88.8% (597/672), and solve 4/6 on IMO 2025, 11/12 on Putnam 2025, and 3/6 on USAMO 2026. This represents state-of-the-art performance for an open-source pipeline at a price point up to 500x less than comparable open-source pipelines.

</details>


### [311] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLEvolve，一个面向端到端机器学习算法发现的 LLM 自演化多智能体框架，通过改进搜索、记忆机制和分层控制提升长程优化能力，并在 MLE-Bench 与数学算法优化任务上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLE 智能体在长程任务中存在分支间信息隔离、搜索过程缺乏记忆、缺少层次化控制等问题，限制了其在科学发现和机器学习工程中的持续自我演化与长期优化能力。

Method: 提出 MLEvolve：将树搜索扩展为 Progressive MCGS，引入图式参考边实现跨分支信息流；采用受熵启发的渐进调度，使搜索从广泛探索逐步转向集中利用；设计 Retrospective Memory，将冷启动领域知识库与动态全局记忆结合，实现任务经验检索与复用；并将战略规划与代码生成解耦，使用自适应编码模式增强长程迭代稳定性。

Result: 在 MLE-Bench 上，MLEvolve 在 12 小时预算下于平均奖牌率、有效提交率等多个指标达到当前最优表现；同时在数学算法优化任务中超过包括 AlphaEvolve 在内的专门算法发现方法，显示出较强跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve 通过跨分支信息共享、经验记忆复用和分层控制机制，显著增强了 LLM 智能体在长程机器学习算法发现任务中的自演化能力与稳定性，并具备跨领域应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [312] [Sequence Reconstruction for Substitution Channel: New Sufficient Conditions and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05454)
*Chen Wang,Eitan Yaakobi,Yiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究序列重构问题，提出一种结合读数数量与读数间距离的新唯一重构充分条件，并给出理论分析与高效重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Levenshtein 框架主要关注保证唯一重构所需的最少 noisy reads 数量，这是一种仅基于读数数量的充分条件。作者希望从更细粒度角度刻画唯一重构条件，即同时利用读数之间的距离信息，以获得更灵活或更强的重构判定框架。

Method: 提出新的唯一重构框架，将 reads 的数量以及 reads 之间的距离共同纳入充分条件；在该框架下进行理论分析，并设计对应的高效重构算法，用于在满足条件时恢复原始码字。

Result: 摘要表明论文给出了新的唯一重构充分条件，并提供了理论保证和相应的高效算法，但未披露具体定理形式、复杂度界或实验/性能指标。

Conclusion: 该工作将序列重构的判定从单纯依赖 reads 数量扩展到结合 reads 间距离的框架，为唯一重构提供了新的理论工具和算法实现路径。

Abstract: In the sequence reconstruction problem, a codeword $\x$ is transmitted through several identical channels where each channel produces a noisy read of $\x$, and the problem is to analyze how to uniquely reconstruct $\x$ based on these noisy reads. Levenshtein has studied the minimum number of reads which guarantees unique reconstruction of $\x$, which is one sufficient condition for unique reconstruction. In this paper, we move on to a different perspective and propose a new framework for unique reconstruction. Our new sufficient condition for unique reconstruction takes both the number of reads and the distances among the reads into consideration. We offer both theoretical analysis and corresponding efficient reconstruction algorithms for our reconstruction framework.

</details>


### [313] [Robust Repair of Reed-Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05573)
*Wilton Kim,Stanislav Kruglik,Gaojun Luo,Han Mao Kiah*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 Reed–Solomon 码在低通信带宽下单节点擦除的鲁棒修复问题，将下载的迹信息视为“repair-trace code”，分析其纠错能力并提出高效修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Guruswami–Wootters 迹修复框架可降低单节点修复带宽，但通常假设辅助节点响应正确。实际分布式存储中辅助节点可能返回错误信息，因此需要研究在存在错误响应时能否可靠修复，以及可纠正错误数与码参数之间的关系。

Method: 作者把辅助节点下载的 trace 集合建模为一个新的码（repair-trace code）。通过用分圆陪集刻画相关多项式的零系数，推导允许纠正给定错误迹数 e 的维度 k 上界，以及关于 k 的最小距离下界。对于 q=2 的情形，利用分圆陪集代表元的显式公式得到单错误纠正的精确最优维度界。同时设计两种鲁棒修复算法：一种基于 BCH 界，另一种基于特征和界以获得更强纠错能力。

Result: 论文给出了 repair-trace code 的距离/维度理论界；在二元基域 q=2 时获得单错误纠正的精确最优维度条件。第一种方案达到 BCH 界保证的纠错能力；第二种方案可接近基于特征和的更强界，但计算复杂度额外增加一个 n 因子。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 Guruswami–Wootters 低带宽迹修复框架可以扩展到存在错误辅助响应的鲁棒场景，并通过代数结构分析明确了 Reed–Solomon 码可鲁棒修复的参数范围，同时提供了理论保证与算法实现之间的权衡。

Abstract: We study the problem of robust repair of a single erasure in Reed--Solomon codes under low communication bandwidth. Focusing on the Guruswami--Wootters trace repair framework, we investigate whether a failed node can be correctly repaired in the presence of erroneous responses from helper nodes. Equivalently, we view the collection of downloaded traces as a code, which we call the repair-trace code. By characterizing the zero coefficients of the associated polynomial in terms of cyclotomic cosets, we derive upper bounds on the dimension $k$ that allow correction of a given number of erroneous traces $e$, as well as lower bounds on the minimum distance as a function of $k$. For the case $q=2$, we exploit explicit formulas for cyclotomic coset representatives to obtain the exact optimal dimension bound for single-error correction. We also propose two efficient robust repair schemes. Our first scheme achieves the error-correction capability guaranteed by the BCH bound. To approach a stronger bound based on character sums, we develop a second scheme that tolerates more errors at the cost of an additional factor $n$ in computational complexity.

</details>


### [314] [Energy Efficiency Optimization for Rotatable Antenna-Enabled Uplink NOMA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05600)
*Yixuan Li,Jun Wang,Hongbo Xu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究可旋转天线辅助的上行NOMA系统，通过联合优化接收波束成形、用户功率分配和天线旋转来最大化能效，并提出基于BCD的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上行NOMA中，地面与空中用户的信道条件差异较大，传统固定天线阵列可能难以充分利用空间自由度。引入可独立旋转的接收天线有望改善信道匹配与多用户区分能力，从而提升系统能效。

Method: 构建以能效最大化为目标的联合优化问题，变量包括接收波束成形、用户发射功率和可旋转天线角度。由于问题非凸且变量耦合，采用块坐标下降框架：接收波束成形用最小均方误差准则更新；功率分配采用分式规划处理；天线旋转通过逐次凸近似求解。

Result: 仿真结果显示，所提出的RA-NOMA方案在能效方面优于多个基准方案，验证了可旋转天线与NOMA联合设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 可旋转天线能够为上行NOMA系统提供额外的空间调控自由度；结合波束成形、功率控制与天线旋转的联合优化，可显著提升地面和空中用户混合场景下的能效表现。

Abstract: This paper investigates a rotatable antenna (RA)-enabled uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) system, where a base station equipped with multiple independently RAs serves both ground and aerial users. Specifically, we formulate an energy efficiency (EE) maximization problem by jointly optimizing receive beamforming, user power allocation, and RA rotation. To make the problem tractable, a new block coordinate descent-based algorithm is developed, in which the receive beamforming is updated via the minimum mean square error criterion, while the power allocation and RA rotation are handled by fractional programming and successive convex approximation. Numerical results demonstrate the EE superiority of the proposed RA-NOMA scheme over several benchmarks.

</details>


### [315] [Automated Proving of Shannon-Type Entropy Inequalities via Fine-Tuned Language Models and Guided Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05729)
*Shing Yin Wong,Shaocheng Liu,Linqi Song,Amin Gohari,Cheuk Ting Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究用小规模微调语言模型结合引导式束搜索，自动证明 Shannon 型熵不等式，并在测试集上取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon 型熵不等式证明通常需要从已知约束中构造非平凡线性组合，本质上是随随机变量数量增长而迅速恶化的组合搜索问题；作者希望探索小规模大语言模型是否能学习原子证明步骤并辅助自动化搜索。

Method: 作者微调 0.6B–1.7B 参数的小模型，使其生成原子证明步骤，并与 guided beam search 结合完成证明搜索；同时比较 GPT-5.5 零样本提示和 Psitip，开展训练上下文长度、数据分布以及束搜索评分启发式的消融实验。

Result: 在包含 n=10 到 15 个变量的 60 个保留测试不等式上，0.6B 微调模型结合树搜索达到 85% 证明成功率；GPT-5.5 零样本仅解决 1.7%，Psitip 解决 33.3%。4096-token 且非偏斜训练分布表现最佳；随机评分将成功率从 83% 降至 23%。

Conclusion: 小规模微调模型在结构化搜索框架中可有效自动化 Shannon 型熵不等式证明；性能关键不在模型规模或更长上下文，而在合适的数据分布、格式稳定性、步骤质量以及有效的搜索评分启发式。

Abstract: Proving Shannon-type entropy inequalities is a fundamental task in information theory that often requires constructing non-trivial linear combinations of known constraints, which is a combinatorial search problem that scales poorly with the number of random variables. We investigate whether small-scale large language models (0.6B--1.7B parameters), fine-tuned on atomic proof steps and combined with guided beam search, can automate this process. On a held-out test set of 60 inequalities spanning n=10 to 15 variables, our 0.6B fine-tuned model achieves an 85\% proof success rate with tree search. GPT-5.5 solves 1.7\% samples under zero-shot prompting while Psitip solves 33.3\% samples. A systematic ablation study across training context length (4096 vs.\ 8192 tokens) and data distribution (n=9-skewed vs not skewed) reveals that a 4096-token not skewed training distribution yields the best performance, with extended context and skewed data providing no marginal benefit. We further identify two dominant failure modes -- format failures and step quality degradation -- and verify that the beam-scoring heuristic is essential via a controlled ablation (random scoring reduces success from 83\% to 23\%).

</details>


### [316] [Compositional Boundaries for Density Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05871)
*Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Ali Darijani,Jürgen Beyerer,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式概率密度融合在层级聚合树中如何保持与执行顺序无关，给出了局部二元加权融合规则可组合性的代数边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式不确定性管理系统常受通信、隐私或调度限制而采用不同聚合树；作者希望最终融合密度只由加权信息源决定，而不受中间节点合并顺序影响。

Method: 将加权概率密度的二元融合形式化为代数组合性问题，分析连续局部段值融合规则、加性输出权重、仅依赖权重的系数条件；比较归一化加权线性池、范数诱导段平衡、端点到候选的 f-散度平衡、全局散度重心以及高斯混合模型中的压缩融合。

Result: 在给定规则类内，顺序不变的层级执行刻画了归一化加权线性池；范数诱导段平衡能实现相应系数。平滑端点到候选 f-散度平衡的二阶展开产生平方根有效权重，说明仅有成对可解性不足以保证调度无关。全局散度重心仍具有加性权重局部极限。高斯混合中，精确融合具可组合性，而逐步压缩只有在非归一化分量测度满足同余条件时才具可组合性。

Conclusion: 论文区分了严格的调度无关融合、全局聚合目标与局部近似启发式方法，指出局部二元融合若要在任意层级结构中保持顺序不变，必须满足强代数组合性约束。

Abstract: Distributed uncertainty-management systems often combine local probabilistic models along aggregation trees chosen by communication, privacy, or scheduling constraints. The final density should depend on the weighted sources, not on the particular order in which intermediate nodes combine them. We study this requirement as an algebraic compositionality problem for binary fusion of weighted probability densities. The central question is when a local fusion rule can be executed hierarchically while remaining order-invariant. We establish a compositional boundary for local segment-valued fusion rules. Within the class of continuous binary rules with additive output weights and weight-only coefficients, order-invariant hierarchical execution characterizes normalized weighted linear pooling; norm-induced segment balancing realizes the corresponding coefficient. Smooth endpoint-to-candidate $f$-divergence balancing has a different local geometry: its quadratic expansion induces square-root effective weights, showing why pairwise solvability alone is insufficient for schedule-independent fusion. We show that this obstruction is local to endpoint-to-candidate binary balancing, whereas global divergence barycenters retain additive-weight local limits. Finally, Gaussian mixtures show how the same issue appears in finite model classes: exact fusion is compositional, whereas stepwise compression is compositional only under a congruence condition on unnormalized component measures. These results distinguish exact schedule-independent fusion from global aggregation objectives and local approximation heuristics.

</details>


### [317] [Double-Directional Wireless Channel Modeling Using Statistics-Aided Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05993)
*Richmond Boamah,Ferdous Pervej*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种统计辅助的机器学习方法，用于生成未来双向无线信道中Top-M多径分量，实现固定维度建模并保持完整时变信道的统计特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向无线信道模型能提供完整传播信息，对真实系统设计重要；但现有随机/确定性模型与多数机器学习方法通常只预测较短时间跨度的信道实现，统计意义有限。同时，多径分量数量会随接收端或交互物体的时空变化而变化，而常规机器学习模型依赖固定输入输出维度，难以直接处理可变数量的MPC。

Method: 作者先从全部多径分量中选择数量远小于总MPC数的Top-M分量，并基于这些分量构造可学习图结构；随后训练混合TimesNet-TimeFilter（TNTF）模型。训练过程中引入信道统计辅助目标，使生成的未来Top-M双向信道实现所计算出的统计量尽可能接近完整时变双向信道的真实统计量。

Result: 在基于合成随机信道模型（SCM）和确定性射线追踪的数据集上进行了大量仿真验证，结果显示该方法相比现有先进基线具有更好的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过固定Top-M多径选择与统计辅助训练，可以缓解MPC数量可变和短期预测统计不足的问题，为长期、统计一致的双向无线信道生成提供有效机器学习框架。

Abstract: The double-directional (DD) wireless channel model is important for realistic system design since it provides complete propagation information. While stochastic and deterministic channel models are widely adopted, and existing machine learning (ML) solutions mostly aim to align future channel realizations, these solutions are often limited to short time spans that may not be statistically significant. Moreover, because the number of multi-path components (MPCs) varies with spatial and temporal variation of the receiver (RX) and/or interacting objects (IOs), typical ML solutions that require fixed, predefined input and output shapes fall short. To curb these limitations, we propose a statistics-aided ML solution that relies on a fixed subset of MPCs selection. More specifically, we first select top-$M$ MPCs, where $M\in\mathbb{Z}^+$ is much smaller than the total number of MPCs, and construct learnable graphs to train our proposed hybrid TimesNet-TimeFilter (TNTF) model. We then use a channel statistics-aided training method to generate future top-M DD channel realizations such that the statistics calculated from these realizations matches closely with those of the actual statistics from the complete time-varying DD channel realizations. We validate the proposed solution using extensive simulations on both synthetic stochastic channel model (SCM)-based and deterministic ray-tracing-based datasets, and demonstrate its effectiveness relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [318] [Adapting Diffusion Language Models for Lossless Pixel-Level Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06273)
*Tianqi Ren,Rongpeng Li,Xianfu Chen,Yingyu Li,Zhifeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 DDM-SSCC：一种基于离散扩散模型的分离式信源-信道编码框架，用于噪声信道下的无损像素级图像传输，并在多个数据集和信道条件下取得更好的精确恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损图像传输要求像素级精确恢复，既需要准确的符号概率建模以支持无损压缩，也需要在噪声信道中可靠传输。传统光栅序自回归编码效率受限，而面向生成的掩码去噪模型与无损算术编码之间存在概率建模与编码流程不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出 DDM-SSCC 框架：将扩散语言模型适配到像素 token 恢复任务，并在双向注意力下进行同步反向算术编码，使一个反向去噪步骤中可编码多个被掩码 token；设计 Halton 引导的去噪顺序以改善空间覆盖，设计 mask-ratio-aware 余弦调度以根据上下文可靠性调整去噪节奏，并引入轻量级温度校准模块校准算术编码所用概率表。

Result: 在 CIFAR10、DIV2K-LR-X4 和 Kodak 数据集上，针对加性白高斯噪声和 Rayleigh 衰落信道进行实验，DDM-SSCC 相比代表性的无损通信与语义通信基线获得更好的精确恢复性能；消融实验验证了去噪顺序、调度策略和校准模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型可作为无损图像传输中的有效信源建模工具；通过双向上下文的渐进式恢复、合理的去噪顺序与概率校准，DDM-SSCC 能提升噪声信道下像素级精确恢复能力。

Abstract: Lossless pixel-level image transmission is a fundamental regime beyond semantic communications, because exact recovery requires both accurate symbol probability modeling and reliable delivery over noisy channels. This paper proposes DDM-SSCC, a discrete-diffusion-model-based separate source-channel coding framework for lossless image transmission. Different from raster-order autoregressive coding, the proposed source codec adapts a diffusion language model to pixel-token restoration and performs synchronized reverse arithmetic coding under bidirectional attention, allowing multiple masked tokens to be coded within one reverse denoising step. This progressive restoration process also yields a more favorable source representation for noisy transmission, since newly restored tokens can serve as bidirectional context in subsequent denoising steps. To bridge the gap between generation-oriented masked denoising and lossless arithmetic coding, we further introduce a Halton-guided denoising order, a mask-ratio-aware cosine schedule, and a lightweight temperature calibration module. These designs respectively improve spatial coverage, adapt the denoising pace to context reliability, and calibrate the probability tables used by arithmetic coding. Experiments on CIFAR10, DIV2K-LR-X4, and Kodak over additive white Gaussian noise and Rayleigh fading channels show that DDM-SSCC achieves better exact-recovery performance than representative lossless and semantic communication baselines, while ablation studies verify the effectiveness of the proposed denoising order, schedule, and calibration modules.

</details>


### [319] [A Spherical Stochastic Geometry Framework for Patrol-Based HAPs Network: Coverage and Energy Efficiency Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06307)
*Mohammad Taha Shah,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向高空平台站（HAPs）巡逻网络的随机几何分析框架，建立两类小圆环 Cox 过程模型，并联合通信覆盖与飞行动力学分析能效优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HAPs 网络分析通常难以同时刻画球面地球上的区域锚定巡逻轨迹、平台空间随机性、有限机队效应以及圆周飞行能耗，因此需要一个可解析的模型来指导巡逻半径、平台密度和速度等部署参数的联合优化。

Method: 论文构建 SCR-PCP 与 SCR-BCP 两种小圆环 Cox 过程模型，证明其各向同性，推导最近锚点、最近巡逻环、最近 HAPs 距离分布及 SCR-BCP 所需的联合服务距离/服务环角分布；在最近 HAPs 关联下，将干扰分解为同环与异环两部分，并推导条件拉普拉斯变换与覆盖概率表达式；进一步结合稳态圆周飞行推进功率模型，定义覆盖能效 CEE，并给出能量最优巡逻半径的解析条件。

Result: 数值结果显示，强度驱动的 SCR-PCP 与有限机队的 SCR-BCP 部署在覆盖与干扰行为上存在本质差异；巡逻几何、平台密度和巡航速度会共同影响覆盖概率与推进能耗，从而决定覆盖能效。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有周期巡逻轨迹的 HAPs 网络提供了统一的球面随机几何建模与性能评估工具，并表明能效最优部署必须在通信覆盖收益和圆周飞行推进成本之间进行权衡。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic-geometry framework for high-altitude platform station (HAPs) networks in which platforms execute cyclic patrol trajectories anchored to designated service regions. We introduce two small-circle ring Cox process models on the spherical Earth. In the small-circle ring Poisson Cox process (SCR-PCP), platforms form one-dimensional Poisson point processes on localized patrol rings, whereas in the small-circle ring binomial Cox process (SCR-BCP), each ring contains a fixed number of uniformly distributed platforms. We establish the isotropy of both models and derive spatial statistics, including the distributions of the nearest-anchor, nearest-ring, and nearest-HAPs distances, together with the joint serving distance and serving ring angle distribution required for SCR-BCP analysis. Building on these results, we derive coverage probability expressions under nearest-HAPs association by decomposing aggregate interference into same-ring and other-ring components and characterizing their conditional Laplace transforms. To account for the flight dynamics of patrol-based HAPs, we integrate a steady circular flight propulsion model with the communication analysis and introduce a coverage energy efficiency (CEE) metric. This yields an analytical condition for the energy-optimal patrol radius that balances coverage performance against the propulsion cost of circular flight. Numerical results reveal fundamental differences between intensity-driven (SCR-PCP) and finite-fleet (SCR-BCP) deployments and demonstrate that patrol geometry, platform density, and cruising velocity should be jointly optimized to achieve energy-efficient HAPs operation.

</details>


### [320] [Reversible double cyclic codes over a chain ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06367)
*Mohd Anwar,Mohd Arif Raza,Mohd Rashid,Muzibur Rahman Mozumder*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限链环 F_q+uF_q（u^2=0）上长度为 (γ,δ) 的双循环码结构，并将其用于构造 DNA 码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双循环码在有限环上的代数结构及其对偶、可逆性与可逆互补性质对编码理论和 DNA 存储编码设计具有应用价值；作者希望建立系统的结构刻画与构造条件。

Method: 论文分析 F_q+uF_q 上双循环码的生成结构，研究其对偶码，给出最小张成集；进一步推导双循环码成为可逆码和可逆互补码的充要条件，并在 F_4+uF_4 上利用这些性质构造 DNA 码。

Result: 得到了长度 (γ,δ) 双循环码的结构描述、对偶码性质、最小张成集，以及可逆和可逆互补双循环码的判定条件；同时构造了一些最优码作为结果支持。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了有限环上双循环码理论，并展示其在 DNA 编码构造中的有效性，尤其为设计满足可逆互补约束的 DNA 码提供了代数方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of double cyclic codes of length $(γ,δ)$ over $\mathbb F_q+u\mathbb F_q, u^2=0$. We also study the dual of double cyclic code of length $(γ,δ)$ and give a minimal spanning set of double cyclic codes. Moreover, we study the necessary and sufficient conditions for a double cyclic code to be reversible and reversible-complement double cyclic code and with the help of these codes, we constructed DNA codes over $\mathbb F_4+u\mathbb F_4, u^2=0$. We also constructed some optimal codes to support our results.

</details>


### [321] [X-Band UAV-enabled Integrated Sensing and Communications for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05262)
*Remon Polus,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究面向车联网的无人机集成感知与通信系统中的最优时间分配问题，在通信速率与感知可靠性约束下平衡感知精度和通信性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机可同时提供空中感知与通信服务，是智能交通系统的重要平台；但在实际飞行约束、信道衰落和目标距离变化下，感知与通信共享时间资源会产生性能权衡，因此需要优化时间分配。

Method: 论文构建了X波段无人机ISaC系统的时间分配优化框架，考虑车联网场景中的最小通信速率、感知可靠性要求、无人机实际约束以及单阴影和双阴影信道模型，分析信道条件与目标距离对资源分配的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明，系统可根据无人机到地面链路条件和目标距离自适应调整感知与通信时间比例；信道质量较差或目标距离变化会显著影响感知-通信资源平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，在无人机辅助智能交通ISaC系统中，合理的时间分配能够在保证通信服务质量和感知可靠性的同时提升整体系统适应性，为智慧出行场景中的无人机资源管理提供了参考。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly considered as aerial platforms capable of providing both sensing and communication services, representing a promising paradigm for intelligent transportation systems. This paper investigates the optimal time allocation for a UAV-enabled integrated sensing and communication (ISaC) system operating in the X-band for vehicular networks. We analyze the trade-off between sensing accuracy and communication performance under practical UAV constraints and fading effects, considering both single-shadowing and double-shadowing channel models. An optimization framework is developed to allocate time between sensing and communication while guaranteeing minimum communication rates and sufficient sensing reliability. Simulation results demonstrate adaptive time allocation strategies, highlighting how UAV-to-ground channel conditions and target distances influence the balance between sensing and communication in smart mobility scenarios.

</details>
